## Review

**6 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck follows a sensible product route (cover, contents, benefits, family, applications, connectivity, optics, specs) with a consistent brand look, and slides 7 and 9 use strong photography with clear columns. However, many pages carry very small body text, such as slides 3, 5, 10, 13 and the spec tables on 18 and 19, which would be hard to read when projected. Several slides lack a clear takeaway: slide 5 has tiny line diagrams with weak A/B/C labelling, slide 14 shows near-identical thumbnails with unreadable labels, and slide 3 is a text wall beside a photo. It works as a leave-behind or a seated reading deck, but it needs substantial revision to present well.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the full-bleed bracket photography with short captions on slide 7, the application-areas layout with skyline line art on slide 9, and the icon-led four-step flow on slide 12.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge body text and cut copy on slides 3, 5, 10, 13 and 14, and redraw the slide 5 mounting diagrams with larger labels tied to their legends.

**What would change the score:** I would turn the benefit text on slide 4 and the optics grid on slide 14 into clearer visual statements, and split the spec tables on 18 and 19 into a readable reference.

_Confidence medium. 22 of 22 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is a sales presentation for the CitySoul LED gen2 urban street luminaire family. It opens with a cover and a contents page, then introduces the product family and its design intent, followed by key benefits such as urban integration, comfort, connectivity and maintenance. The middle sections cover the family overview with its two sizes and mounting options, the bracket and pole range, and the application areas for city centre, residential and traffic routes. It then turns to connectivity through the Interact smart lighting platform and the Service tag QR-code maintenance app, with a step-by-step installation and maintenance flow. The LEDgine optics and engine section explains standardised light distributions, followed by a product specifications section with dimensions and technical data. The deck closes with a company and brand overview and a contact page. Slides worth studying include the family overview diagram (slide 5), the bracket comparison photography (slide 7), the application areas layout (slide 9), and the Service tag installation icon sequence (slide 12).

## Questions about this deck

**What does the Signify CitySoul LED gen2 deck say about Signify Interact smart lighting?**

Slide 10 says that with Interact, it is easier than ever to realize the benefits of smart lighting: higher energy savings, improved space utilization, and easy maintenance. It lists general benefits such as open APIs, no additional DALI control wires needed, and remote control and monitoring.

**How many slides does the Signify CitySoul LED gen2 presentation deck have?**

The Signify CitySoul LED gen2 deck has 22 pages. Slide 2 is the contents page, listing eight sections from Meet CitySoul LED gen2 through Product specifications.

**What mounting heights does the Signify CitySoul LED gen2 deck give for the mini and large post-top luminaires?**

Slide 19 states that the BPP530 (mini) post-top luminaire has a mounting height of 3.5 to 6 m, and the BPP531 (large) has a mounting height of 6 to 9 m. Slide 5 also says the Large version can be used in applications with mounting heights of up to 12 meters.

**What are the installation steps in the Signify Service tag guide for CitySoul LED gen2?**

Slide 12 lists four installation steps: download the Service tag app, register the luminaire by scanning its QR code, create a group QR code for multiple luminaires, and scan the luminaire QR code to access the installation manual. The same slide also covers maintenance steps for spare parts identification and spare driver programming.

**Text**

Meet CitySoul LED gen2
CitySoul LED gen2 is one of the most versatile and
inspirational urban street lighting families designed by
Signify to date.
This highly efficient range delivers excellent lighting levels
whilst also providing the right ambiance for all urban
application areas, from the outskirts of the city right
through to the city center.
By evolving the modularity of the CitySoul family and adding
new innovations like the Lyre and the Accent bracket,
Signify has made this range the ideal toolbox for every
urban context.
The design is flatter, completely round, and the transitions
with the spigot and bracket entirely flush, thereby giving
your cityscape a coherent, elegant and discreet identity.
Designed around its LED engine, CItySoul LED gen2 is highly
efficient and easy to maintain. It comes in two sizes and is
suitable for side-entry, post-top, catenary and suspended
mounting.
3
Signify CitySoul LED gen2

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: I would turn the benefit text on slide 4 and the optics grid on slide 14 into clearer visual statements, and split the spec tables on 18 and 19 into a readable reference.

**Text**

Key benefits of CitySoul LED gen2 / Easy urban integration / The CitySoul LED gen2 family is a modular / range in which luminaires, brackets and / poles work together as a unified design / concept. With flush transitions and clean / lines, CitySoul LED gen2 is a discreet, / elegant fit for any urban context. / Ready for connectivity / The luminaire comes with one or two SR / (system-ready) sockets, which makes the / luminaire future ready. What this means is / that CitySoul LED gen2 is ready to be paired / with both standalone and advanced control / and lighting software applications such as / Interact City from Signify. / Comfort and ambiance / CitySoul LED gen2 does more than / producing the required lighting levels. / Thanks to diffusing tool and several light / recipes, CitySoul LED gen2 adds a sense of / comfort to city lighting and enhances the / nighttime ambiance. / Improved maintenance / Each CitySoul LED gen2 luminaire is uniquely / identifiable, thanks to the Signify Service tag / application. With a simple scan of a QR code, / you gain instant access to the luminaire / configuration, making maintenance and / programming operations faster and easier, / and enabling you to create your digital / library of lighting assets and spare parts. / 4 / Signify CitySoul LED gen2 / (s)ignify

**Layout**

Two-by-two grid of benefit blocks below a title; each block has a green outlined circular icon on the left and a bold heading with body text to its right. The left column starts at x about 13.6% and the right column at about 61.8%; icons sit at x about 3% and 51%. The title sits top-left at x 3.4%, y 6.1%, with a left margin of about 3.4%. Footer slide number and footer text sit bottom-left at y about 92.5%; the logo sits bottom-right, ending at about 97.4% from the left.

**Visuals**

Four circular line icons, each a green (#19D27F) outline circle about 8.8% wide with thin green line artwork inside: top-left icon shows a stacked city building outline with small window dots and a vertical pole-like base; top-right icon shows three concentric Wi-Fi style arcs above a small open circle dot, representing connectivity; bottom-left icon shows a tall pole with a lamp head at top and three short light rays beneath it, inside a rounded-corner frame; bottom-right icon shows a crossed wrench and screwdriver. Icons are outline-only, no fills, flat, no shadows. Bottom-right logo is the Signify wordmark '(s)ignify' in green (#19D27F) with a small circled s glyph, about 7.5% wide.

**Design**

Background: Solid charcoal #303034. Dark charcoal background (#303034) with no gradient or texture. All titles and body text are white (#FFFFFF); headings are bold white. Accent colour is green (#19D27F) used for icons and logo. Title typeface is a geometric display sans with quirky letterforms (Signify Raisonne DemiBold, or closest geometric sans, sans-serif), title text height about 5.2% of slide height. Body and headings use Signify Relative, a geometric grotesque sans-serif; headings bold, body book weight, body text height about 3.0% of slide height, footer about 2.0%. Line spacing for body is about 1.2. Icon circles have 1 to 2 px green stroke, no corner radius on circles, no shadows.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a clean technical diagram with lettered callouts to show the many mounting variants on one page.
- Review: However, many pages carry very small body text, such as slides 3, 5, 10, 13 and the spec tables on 18 and 19, which would be hard to read when projected.
- Review: Several slides lack a clear takeaway: slide 5 has tiny line diagrams with weak A/B/C labelling, slide 14 shows near-identical thumbnails with unreadable labels, and slide 3 is a text wall beside a photo.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text and cut copy on slides 3, 5, 10, 13 and 14, and redraw the slide 5 mounting diagrams with larger labels tied to their legends.

**Text**

CitySoul family overview
The discreet CitySoul LED gen2 is the most versatile urban street lighting family.
CitySoul LED gen2 offers three brackets (right curve, east, graphic), for different urban areas.
An elegant lyre for more urban focused applications. An accent bracket that allows you
to create the most elegant spiral configurations.
Two versions
A CitySoul LED gen2 Mini
Has a smaller volume - a
perfect match for lower
heights.
B CitySoul LED gen2 Large
Lets you benefit from its
larger volume in applications
with mounting heights of up
to 12 meters.
Suspended mountings
A Dedicated suspended
mounting for Ø 76 mm
B Catenary / Standard suspended
mounting
Direct mountings
A MBA wall mounting
B Accent
C MBA direct mounting
Side-entry mountings
A Standard side-entry
mounting for Ø 42-60 mm
B MBA side-entry mounting
C Dedicated side-entry mounting
Top mountings
A Post-top for Ø 60-76 mm
B Double post-top for Ø 60 mm
C MBA mounting for Ø 60-76 mm
D Lyre for Ø 60 mm
5
Signify CitySoul LED gen2
(s)ignify

**Layout**

Single-column title block at top left with a large intro text block below it, followed by five illustration columns of unequal width spanning the lower half, each with a caption and lettered key beneath. Left margin about 4 percent, title top at about 6 percent; footer with page number and deck name sits at about 92 percent height, with the Signify logo right-aligned at the bottom right edge (about 90 to 96 percent width). Text is left-aligned throughout.

**Visuals**

Five technical mounting diagrams and two product renders, drawn in flat grey-and-black line and fill style on white, with blue letter callouts (A, B, C, D) placed beside each part. Column 1 'Two versions': two product photographs of street luminaires (A: CitySoul LED gen2 Mini, a small grey-black head on a short arm, upper; B: CitySoul LED gen2 Large, a larger oval grey-black head, lower) with thin vertical and horizontal grey axis lines at left and bottom. Column 2 'Suspended mountings': A a light grey horizontal bracket arm with a grey-black luminaire hanging beneath it; B a catenary cable running horizontally with a central white hanger and a black-grey luminaire below. Column 3 'Direct mountings': three small side views: A wall plate with arm and luminaire, B accent bracket with luminaire, C a diagonal MBA direct mount with luminaire. Column 4 'Side-entry mountings': three side views on a vertical post: A a standard side-entry arm with luminaire, B an MBA side-entry arm with luminaire, C a dedicated side-entry arm with a thin lattice support and luminaire. Column 5 'Top mountings': four views on a horizontal ground line: A post-top luminaire on a grey post, B double post-top with two luminaires on a central post, C MBA mounting with luminaire on a short arm, D lyre with a diagonal dark arm and luminaire. Luminaires are dark grey (#3A3A3E) with light grey (#B8BCC0) highlights; no photographs of people; no overlays or tints; diagrams are not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF, no gradient or pattern.

**Layout**

Two-zone split slide: the left half is white and the right half is light grey (#D5D5D5), divided at the vertical centre line. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top and 3.4% from the left edge. Two luminaire photographs sit side by side in each half, with a bold model name and white-range text centred beneath each. The footer (page number and blue deck name) sits bottom-left, and the Signify logo sits bottom-right on the grey half.

**Visuals**

Four product photographs of round outdoor LED street luminaires, shown head-on from the front, with the mounting bracket and arm adaptor visible at the bottom of each. Left pair (white half): CitySoul LED gen2 mini 10/20 LED, a smaller head with a rectangular LED board of about 4 columns of LEDs; CitySoul LED gen2 mini 30/40 LED, a larger LED board with more LEDs and a small round adjustment knob at lower right. Right pair (grey half): CitySoul LED gen2 large 40 LED, a larger head with a dense LED array; CitySoul LED gen2 large 60/80 LED, the largest head with a dense full-width LED array. Each photo has a dark charcoal housing ring, a white diffuser disc and light grey background, lit evenly from the front with soft shadows, shown full frame without crop, boxed by nothing and with no overlay or tint. Signify logo at bottom right: the circled S mark followed by the lowercase wordmark 'ignify' in dark green-black.

**Design**

Background: Split background: white #FFFFFF on the left half and light grey #D5D5D5 on the right half. Clean corporate product sheet. Background is white on the left half and light grey #D5D5D5 on the right half. Title in Signify Raisonne DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, about 5.2% of slide height, colour #303034. Captions in SignifyRelative, a grotesque sans-serif: model names in Bold and wattage lines in Book, about 2.6% of slide height, colour #303034, centred. Footer in SignifyRelative Book, about 2.0% of slide height, colour #1432FF (electric blue). No shapes, rules, shadows, gradients or rounded corners; the only graphic division is the change in background tone at the centre line.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Places five bracket photographs in a single row with consistent captions, letting the product speak through its setting.
- Review: The deck follows a sensible product route (cover, contents, benefits, family, applications, connectivity, optics, specs) with a consistent brand look, and slides 7 and 9 use strong photography with clear columns.
- Worth keeping: Keep the full-bleed bracket photography with short captions on slide 7, the application-areas layout with skyline line art on slide 9, and the icon-led four-step flow on slide 12.

**Text**

Brackets / Right Curve bracket / With its slender shapes and / silhouettes, the Right / Curve bracket has an / attractive aerial feeling / that will integrate perfectly / into classical / environments. / Lyre bracket / Any location where ambiance / lighting is needed to create a / pleasant, comfortable / atmosphere is the ideal / setting for the Lyre bracket, / the Lyre dedicated pole and / the CitySoul LED gen2 / luminaire. / East bracket / For modern areas and / streets, the East bracket is / a perfect fit. With its pure / and lean shape, it gives / neighborhoods a / sophisticated and / contemporary feel. / UrbanWave bracket / A tubular curved shape that / looks like a wave and will fit / perfectly with CitySoul LED / gen2 luminaires in / contemporary urban settings. / Graphic bracket / Designed to reference / traditional architectural steel / frame structures, the Graphic / bracket and CitySoul LED gen2 / luminaire offer a modernized / look of a typically industrial / form factor. / 7 / Signify CitySoul LED gen2 / signify (logo)

**Layout**

Five equal-width photographs run edge to edge across the slide in a single row, full-bleed horizontally from the left to the right edge, spanning about 15% to 68.5% of the slide height. The title Brackets sits top-left at about 6% from the top and 3.4% from the left edge. Each photo has one caption block beneath it, left-aligned with a left inset of about 2.9% to 20.6% of the width, arranged in five columns. The footer has the blue page number 7 at left and the blue footer text Signify CitySoul LED gen2 beside it, and the Signify logo sits bottom right at about 90% from the left.

**Visuals**

Five photographs of street lighting poles in urban and suburban settings, arranged left to right. (1) Right Curve bracket: a street lamp with an arm and luminaire on a tall slender pole, in front of a town of stone buildings with a mountain slope behind; a person walks a dog on the road and a dark car is parked at left; bright daylight, blue sky, trees on the right; cropped to a tall rectangle. (2) Lyre bracket: a tall pole with a curved Lyre arm and luminaire beside a stone retaining wall and clipped hedges, with a grey-roofed house and trees behind; soft overcast daylight; greens and beige; cropped to a tall rectangle. (3) East bracket: a slender pole with a luminaire in a street with a yellow building, a parked silver car, a woman in a red skirt and a man in a blue shirt walking on the pavement, young trees, and a traffic light at left; warm daylight; yellow, grey and white; cropped to a tall rectangle. (4) UrbanWave bracket: a pole with a curved wave-shaped arm and luminaire at a road with a traffic light, road signs, a parked bicycle and bollards, apartment blocks and blue sky with clouds; bright daylight; blue, grey and white; cropped to a tall rectangle. (5) Graphic bracket: a tall pole with a luminaire at dusk beside a road, with a low sun glowing through haze, bare trees and a hedge; warm orange and amber light; dark silhouette foreground; cropped to a tall rectangle. The photographs are butted together with no gaps or borders. The measured image boxes in the file do not line up one-to-one with the five visible photographs, so the visible positions are used here. Signify logo: the word signify in dark #303034 lowercase with a circled s symbol at its left, bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title and body text are dark charcoal #303034. The footer page number and footer text are in blue #1432FF, the only accent colour. Typefaces are geometric sans-serif: the title uses Signify Raisonne DemiBold, a heavy geometric sans, at about 5.2% of slide height; captions use Signify Relative, with Bold for column headings and Book for body text at about 1.85% of slide height. Photographs are rectangular with square corners and no shadow or border. No lines or shape outlines are used.

### Slide 8

**Text**

Poles and brackets / Ocean curve pole / An elegant curved shape with an / articulated interface dedicated to the / luminaire and an optional LED top point / to further highlight a major street‘s / design signature. / Double post-top / This compact and straightforward / design is the perfect solution for / urban contexts in which performance / lighting is the primary requisite. / Accent brackets and / Ocean straight pole / A versatile and stylish solution that / can feature up to 4 luminaires / mounted in an attractive spiral / configuration. The optional LED top / point will highlight your environment‘s / design signature at night. / Catenary / The Catenary bracket allows the / CitySoul gen2 LED luminaire to fit into / streets and on path where decluttering / is a necessity – like shopping districts / and carfree zones. / 8 / Signify CitySoul LED gen2

**Layout**

Full-width layout with a title at top left and a four-column grid below. Four photographs sit edge to edge in equal columns (about 0 to 25, 25 to 50, 50 to 75 and 75 to 100 percent of width) spanning from about 14.6 to 77.4 percent of height, each with a bold caption block beneath it, left-aligned at the column's left edge with about 3.4 percent margin. The title sits top left at x 3.4, y 6.1. The footer runs along the bottom at about y 92.5 with the page number and series name in blue at left and the Signify logo at bottom right, about x 90 and y 91.

**Visuals**

Four photographs of street lighting, each filling its column with no gaps and no borders. Column 1 (0 to 25 percent): a tall curved twin-arm pole with two luminaires against a clear deep blue sky, trees and a road below, cool daylight. Column 2 (25 to 50 percent): a single straight dark grey post-top pole with a twin round luminaire head against a pale grey-blue sky, with compact trees, a parked white van and parked vehicles behind, bright daylight. Column 3 (50 to 75 percent): a night view of a slim pole with a spiral cluster of five lit white luminaires against a near-black sky, beside a large white curved modern building with blue-lit glazing, dark tones with bright white light sources. Column 4 (75 to 100 percent): a bright daytime street of cream and red-brick historic facades with a suspended Catenary bracket luminaire on a wire overhead, leafy green trees and a clear blue sky with white clouds. No overlays or tints; the photos are rectangular, not rounded. File-measured image boxes overlap the columns: 0.0 / 14.6 / 25.0 / 63.0 (100 by 141 px); 25.0 / 14.6 / 25.1 / 62.8 (208 by 293 px); 21.3 / 14.8 / 21.4 / 53.5 (416 by 585 px); 0.0 / 14.8 / 21.3 / 53.6 (397 by 562 px); 42.7 / 14.8 / 42.6 / 53.5 (829 by 586 px); 50.2 / 14.6 / 49.8 / 62.8 (415 by 293 px). A small logo at 89.9 / 91.0 is the Signify wordmark with its circled S mark in dark ink.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in a geometric sans-serif demibold, dark charcoal #303034, large (about 5.2 percent of slide height, about 7 percent cap height). Caption headings in a bold geometric sans, #303034, about 1.85 percent of slide height. Caption body in a book-weight geometric sans, #303034, same size, with generous leading of about 2.5 percent of slide height. Footer page number and series name in a book-weight sans, bright blue #1432FF, about 2.0 percent of slide height. Photos are flat, untreated, with no shadows, borders, corner radii or gradients. No rules or decorative shapes.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Presents the application areas on a dark background with a city skyline illustration and three short columns, keeping text minimal.
- Review: The deck follows a sensible product route (cover, contents, benefits, family, applications, connectivity, optics, specs) with a consistent brand look, and slides 7 and 9 use strong photography with clear columns.
- Worth keeping: Keep the full-bleed bracket photography with short captions on slide 7, the application-areas layout with skyline line art on slide 9, and the icon-led four-step flow on slide 12.

**Text**

Application areas
CitySoul LED gen2 is part of our Urban Inspiration lighting portfolio. It incorporates all the necessary functional aspects of lighting whilst also having a strong focus on the emotional value of light.
In this way, it helps to create the right ambiance, enhance people’s well-being, and make the city more livable. For every application we have several solutions that will inspire you and enhance your city, while also fulfilling all your specific requirements.
City center
• Side street
• Square, park and playground
• Cycle path and footpath
• Shopping and pedestrian area
Residential area
• Residential street
• Cycle path and footpath
• Square, park and playground
• Parking area
Traffic route
• Boulevard and avenue
• Main urban/ access roads
• Roundabout
9
Signify CitySoul LED gen2

**Layout**

Two-column split: a dark text panel fills the left part of the slide (about 62.5% of width) and a stack of three photographs fills the right part (about 37.5%), running full-bleed to the top, right and bottom edges. The title sits top-left at x 3.4%, y 6.1%, with a left margin of about 3.2% of width. Below it are two intro paragraphs, then a thin-line skyline illustration resting on a horizontal rule at about 58.5% of height, then three bullet columns (City center, Residential area, Traffic route) starting at x 3.8%, 23.8% and 44.7%. The footer sits bottom-left at y 92.5%: page number 9 followed by 'Signify CitySoul LED gen2'. No logo and no slide-number box on the right.

**Visuals**

Three photographs stacked on the right, all rectangular, unrounded and without borders, full-bleed to the slide edge. (1) Top photo: a seaside street in daylight; on the left a large multi-storey half-timbered (Norman-style) apartment block with steep slate roofs and wooden balconies, a white picket fence and low hedges in front; a grey road runs diagonally to the right with several parked silver and grey cars, a motorcyclist, tall grey lampposts and a blue-white sign on the right edge; blue sky with white cumulus clouds; framing wide, slightly low; full daylight; dominant colours blue, cream, brown, grey. Visible part is roughly the top 32% of the slide height; the frame extends under the marina photo. (2) Middle photo: a marina with several white and blue sailboats with masts raised out of the water on dry-dock cradles, a white motor boat on the left, tall palm trees in the foreground and middle, clipped round shrubs along a low wall, a dark parked car at right, a tall grey lamppost, hazy hills on the horizon, pale blue sky; bright daylight; dominant colours blue, white, green, beige; it sits over the top photo and under the bottom photo, visible from about 32% to 75.5% of slide height. (3) Bottom photo: a street of low red-tiled roofs and brick houses under blue sky with light cloud, two grey lampposts, a white modern building on the right with a green and yellow shop sign reading QUESNOY Primeur, a blue accessibility sign on a pole, a black railing along the bottom edge; visible from about 75.5% to the bottom of the slide; dominant colours blue, red-brown, white. Skyline illustration: a single-weight outline drawing in bright green #00E487 and light grey strokes on the dark background, sitting on a thin grey ground line at about 58.5% of height. From left to right: a tall rounded egg-shaped tower with horizontal ring lines, a block of flats with a grid of windows and an angled tower beside it, a small pedestrian figure, a narrow conifer tree, a round-headed tree with a bench, a cyclist, two round-crown trees and a playground frame with a slide, a pedestrian figure, a building with arched windows and a door, a car, a conifer tree and a round-crown tree, then a row of small cars, vans and trucks at the right end. Horizontal rule: a 1 px light grey line at about 58.5% height from the left edge to about 62.5% of width.

**Design**

Background: Solid charcoal #303034 across the whole slide.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Review: However, many pages carry very small body text, such as slides 3, 5, 10, 13 and the spec tables on 18 and 19, which would be hard to read when projected.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text and cut copy on slides 3, 5, 10, 13 and 14, and redraw the slide 5 mounting diagrams with larger labels tied to their legends.

**Text**

Interact for smart lighting
This is all you need for your
smart lighting system
With Interact, it's easier than ever to realize the benefits of smart lighting: higher energy savings, improved space utilization, easy maintenance — and a big step towards achieving sustainability goals.
General benefits
• Open APIs for an easy integration in other city management systems
• No additional (DALI) control wires needed
• Quick to install and ready to use in a few clicks
Sustainable
• Enable instant energy and cost savings
• Reduce CO2 emission and material waste
Smart
• Benefit from remote control and monitoring and reporting capabilities
• Explore the options to find further energy savings by dimming, time schedules and grouping light points
• Use data to make better informed decisions more quickly
Safe
• Set the right conditions to ensure comfortable lighting and increased vision
• Get informed quickly to enable timely and cost-effective maintenance
Discover Interact for area lighting
10 Signify CitySoul LED gen2
CitySoul LED gen2 with integrated sensor and Interact node
+
Software applications

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: a white left column (about 0 to 53% of width) holds the title, intro paragraph, four benefit groups with bullets, a link and the footer; a light grey right panel (about 53% to 100% of width, full height) holds a centred stack of heading, product photo with badge, caption, blue plus sign, application icon circle, caption and Signify logo. Title sits top-left at about 6% from top and 3.4% from left margin; the left column text is left-aligned to the same margin. Footer page number and title sit bottom-left at about 92.5% from top; the Signify logo sits bottom-right of the grey panel.

**Visuals**

Product photograph (cutout, not cropped, boxed on the grey panel with no border): a dark charcoal LED street lamp (CitySoul LED gen2) with a vertical post on the left edge of the frame and a flat, elongated luminaire head at top, its underside a pale glowing translucent lens; lit evenly from above, neutral studio lighting; dominant colours dark grey #3A3B3F and pale grey lens; small black Interact ready badge overlaid on the lower right of the post area. Interact ready badge: a small rectangle, near-black #1E1E1E background, with a green (#2BD67B approx.) circled S mark and white lowercase text 'interact' over green 'ready'. Blue plus sign: a bold geometric cross, solid blue #1432FF, centred under the lamp caption. Software applications icon: a thin-outlined pink-red (#F0385E approx.) circle, about 2 px stroke, containing six small line icons arranged around the circle: a laptop at top, a server/rack unit at upper left, a smartphone at upper right, a wallet-like box at centre, a tablet at lower left, and a small monitor/device at bottom, each joined to the circle by short arc gaps. Signify logo: black wordmark 'signify' with a stylised S in a circle, bottom right, small.

**Design**

Background: white left half and light grey #EAEAEB right panel. Title and body text colour #303034 (near-black charcoal). Accent: pink-red bullets #FF375F; blue #1432FF for the plus sign, link, and footer. Typefaces: title in SignifyRaisonne-DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif with semi-bold weight; body in SignifyRelative-Book, a geometric sans-serif regular weight; bold in SignifyRelative-Bold for plus and link. Title height about 5.2% of slide height (cap height about 3.6%); body text about 2.2% of slide height; grey panel heading about 3.9%; caption text about 2.4%; footer about 2.0%. Lines are plain, no rules or shapes, no corner radii, no shadows, no gradients. Bullets are small pink dots. Links are underlined.

### Slide 11

**Text**

Signify Service tag
A unique QR code-based LED luminaire asset identification system
Providing detailed information on specification and spare parts, simply by scanning the code on the packaging or luminaire with the Signify Service tag app.
The system simplifies the installation and maintenance of lighting infrastructure by utilizing cloud technology.
Service tag also plays a key role in the circular design of our lighting solutions, as it extends their service life and reduces maintenance efforts, ensuring efficient servicing and avoiding waste.
Your benefits at a glance:
Learn more at signify.com/global/service-tag
Easy installation and commissioning
Simplified maintenance
Asset management
11
Signify CitySoul LED gen2

**Layout**

Two-column split: a white text column on the left (about 0 to 51.6% of width) and a full-height photograph panel on the right (51.6% to 100% width, top 62.7% of height), with a green link band beneath the photo. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top and left margin about 3.4%, all text left-aligned. A full-width charcoal band across the lower part (71.6% to 89.4% of height) holds three benefit items, each an icon followed by a two-line label. The footer strip below holds the slide number at left, the footer text in blue beside it, and the Signify wordmark at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Photograph in the upper right panel: a person's hands hold a black Sony tablet in landscape orientation, the screen showing a blue Signify 'Service parts' app screen with a luminaire technical drawing at upper left, a list of service parts with thumbnails and blue buttons, and a 'Shopping basket' item at lower left. Behind it, a dark grey LED luminaire with a lit white LED array sits out of focus, and a tan cardboard box fills the lower left. The grey pavement is blurred at top. Daylight lighting, soft focus on background, sharp tablet in the centre. Dominant colours: grey, black, tan brown, royal blue screen. Full-bleed within its panel, not cropped by any shape, no overlay or tint. A small rounded white card (Signify service tag icon) overlaps the photo's lower right: a dark outlined price-tag shape with a green QR code, and the green lowercase 'signify' wordmark with a circular s beneath it. Three green outline line icons in circles inside the charcoal band: a light bulb inside a plug-like device with a cable (Easy installation and commissioning); crossed wrench and screwdriver (Simplified maintenance); a document with lines and a folded corner (Asset management). Footer logo at bottom right: black Signify wordmark with circular s.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF solid background with left column white and right panel photograph. White background across the slide. Title in charcoal #303034 in a geometric display sans (Signify Raisonne DemiBold style), medium-heavy weight, about 5.2% of slide height. Body bullets in charcoal #303034 in a geometric sans book weight, about 3.0% of slide height, with generous line spacing. Bullet glyphs and footer text and link in blue #1432FF. Bold labels in #303034 for 'Your benefits at a glance:' and 'Learn more at'. Green accent #00E487 for the link band and icon outlines. Charcoal #303034 band with white bold labels about 2.6% of height. Photo full-bleed, no border. Icon card has rounded corners and a soft shadow. Footer text about 2.0% of height.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Breaks the installation process into four numbered steps with matching icons, making a procedure easy to follow at a glance.
- Worth keeping: Keep the full-bleed bracket photography with short captions on slide 7, the application-areas layout with skyline line art on slide 9, and the icon-led four-step flow on slide 12.

_Long standard legal text on this slide is summarised, not transcribed; its exact words are in the measured list below._

**Text**

Signify Service tag: Installation
Maintenance
#1. Download the app
Download the Service tag app from the App store or Play store on your mobile device (iOS or Android).
#2. Register the luminaire
Register the luminaire by simply scanning the QR code on the packaging or luminaire. The system automatically identifies the type of each luminaire.
#3. Create a group QR code
If you have multiple luminaires, it’s possible to use the Service tag app to combine individual luminaires into a group. Each group will have its own unique group QR code.
#4. Luminaire information
Scan the QR code on your luminaire for easy access to the installation manual.
#1. Spare parts identification
Use the Service tag app to view a luminaire’s spare parts, which contains all necessary information for quick ordering.
#2. Spare parts programming
Quickly program a spare driver using the Service tag app—without needing a faulty driver on hand. Scan the QR code of a luminaire with your device, put your mobile device on the spare driver, press “Program” and the spare driver receives the right settings for this specific luminaire.
Learn more at
signify.com/global/service-tag
signify

**Layout**

Two-column grid on a white field with a light grey right-hand panel (x 66.8 to 100) holding the Maintenance column; the left area is a 2×2 grid of numbered steps (columns centred at about x 17, x 51 and x 17 and 51 respectively, rows starting at y 14.6 and y 48.3). The title sits top-left at x 3.4, y 6.1 with a left margin of about 3.4% and the Maintenance heading is centred in the grey panel at the same height. Each step is a centred stack of icon, bold heading and centred body text. A green Learn more band sits bottom-left (y 84.6 to 96.2) and the Signify logo sits bottom-right of the grey panel at about x 90, y 91. No footer, slide number or date is visible. Body text runs to about 170 words and is centred within each column.

**Visuals**

Six dark circular icons in white line art, each about 120 px in diameter, arranged in two rows above and beside their headings: (1) a smartphone outline with speaker slot and home button, above “#1. Download the app”; (2) a lightbulb with a check mark in a small circle at its lower right, above “#2. Register the luminaire”; (3) a network or org-chart diagram of one rectangle over three small squares connected by lines, above “#3. Create a group QR code”; (4) a lightbulb inside a circle of two curved arrows, above “#4. Luminaire information”; (5) a magnifying glass with two small diagonal strokes on the lens, above “#1. Spare parts identification”; (6) a touch panel showing a small chart with a hand pointing at it, above “#2. Spare parts programming”. Each icon is flat, with no outline or shadow. One app tile: a white rounded square with a green-outlined price-tag shape containing a small QR code, with the word signify at its bottom, on the green band. One Signify logo (black s-in-circle mark and wordmark) at bottom right of the grey panel. No photographs; no charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a full-height light grey #F2F2F2 panel covering x 66.8 to 100. Two-tone background: white on the left, light grey #F2F2F2 panel on the right third. Accent green #00E487 band at bottom left. Titles and body text in charcoal #303034; step numbers #1432FF blue. Titles use Signify Raisonne DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, at about 5.2% of slide height; headings use Signify Relative Bold at about 2.6%; body uses Signify Relative Book at about 2.2%. Line spacing about 2.7% of slide height in body paragraphs. Icons are flat dark grey circles #3A3A3F with white line art. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients; the app tile has a small corner radius.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Review: However, many pages carry very small body text, such as slides 3, 5, 10, 13 and the spec tables on 18 and 19, which would be hard to read when projected.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text and cut copy on slides 3, 5, 10, 13 and 14, and redraw the slide 5 mounting diagrams with larger labels tied to their legends.

**Text**

LEDgine
Standardized optics
- A perfect application fit
A wide range of light distributions ensure a / perfect fit for many applications.
The optics offer flexibility, enabling / standardization over applications with a good / performance across a wide range of / geometries – as well as design parameters / such as tilt and overhang. The optics comply / with national and European road lighting / standards.
Standard engines
- High performance across portfolio
Using a standard engine across different / luminaire ranges means you can optimally / benefit from the latest LED upgrades without / changing light distributions, so design / continuity is assured. Standard flux packages / are pre-defined across product ranges. Flux / and thereby energy minimization is achieved / by using best possible lumen maintenance (up / to L98 or CLO solutions). The LEDgine / optimized engine benefits from more LEDs / per area contributing to more compact and / cost effective solutions.
Standard engines minimize spare components / which are easy to configure by using our / Service tag application.
Tailor-made solutions
- Tuning to project preferences
We can support you with our exclusive online / tools to customize solutions which of course / can become your standard!
Using a variety of LED counts it enables to / build the exact required flux in a perfect / balance between energy consumption, / luminaire cost/type and operational life. / Based on project parameters the best optical / fit can be selected and when required light / distributions can be customized for best / application fit and to maximize energy savings.
13
Signify CitySoul LED gen2
Signify logo

**Layout**

Three equal columns of text sit below a single title row on a white page with generous margins. The title 'LEDgine' is top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6% from the top, and a green LEDgine logo badge sits top-right at about 84% from the left. Each column has an icon at the top, then a bold heading, a bold dash-prefixed sub-line, and body text; columns start at x about 4.4%, 36.1% and 67.7%. The footer holds a blue page number '13' and the deck name at bottom-left, with the Signify logo bottom-right. There are no visible slide-edge bleeds.

**Visuals**

Three line icons, each inside an outlined circle, in a row under the title. Column 1 icon: a cog/gear with six teeth and a central ring, drawn in dark grey outline, centred in a circle about 7% wide. Column 2 icon: a small network diagram of five open circles (nodes) joined by thin zigzag line segments, centred in a circle. Column 3 icon: two speech bubbles (upper-left bubble with three horizontal lines, right bubble with a plain interior) above a simple outline figure of a person with head and shoulders, centred in a circle. All three circles share the same dark grey outline stroke of about 1.5 px and white interior. Top-right green LEDgine logo: a rounded rectangle, solid mint green, containing a 3 by 4 grid of dark dots at left and the dark wordmark 'LEDgine' at right. Bottom-right Signify logo: the word 'signify' in black lowercase with a small circled 's' mark at left. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradients or shadows. Title 'LEDgine' is set in a geometric display sans (Signify Raisonne DemiBold style) in dark grey #303034 at about 5.2% of slide height. Column headings are a bold geometric sans (Signify Relative Bold) in #303034 at about 3.0% of slide height. Dash sub-lines are bold, about 2.2%. Body text is a regular-weight geometric sans (Signify Relative Book) in #303034 at about 2.2%, with about 2.9% line spacing and left alignment. Bold is used for inline emphasis within body text. Accent colours: mint green #00E582 for the logo badge, and blue #1432FF for the footer page number and deck name. Icons are dark grey #303034 outlines. Lines and shapes are thin and unfilled; no corner radius on the text, a small rounded corner on the green logo badge.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: Several slides lack a clear takeaway: slide 5 has tiny line diagrams with weak A/B/C labelling, slide 14 shows near-identical thumbnails with unreadable labels, and slide 3 is a text wall beside a photo.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text and cut copy on slides 3, 5, 10, 13 and 14, and redraw the slide 5 mounting diagrams with larger labels tied to their legends.
- What would change the score: I would turn the benefit text on slide 4 and the optics grid on slide 14 into clearer visual statements, and split the spec tables on 18 and 19 into a readable reference.

**Text**

14 Signify CitySoul LED gen2
signify

**Layout**

Single white slide with the title top-left at about 6 percent from the top, left margin about 3.4 percent. Content is in two columns: the left column holds Luminance classes (M) in the upper area and Illuminance classes (P, C) below it with a blue arrow caption; the right column holds Distributions for dedicated applications, Night preservation, and Distributions for special user needs. Footer with page number 14 and blue series name sits bottom-left, Signify logo bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Thirty-six thumbnail schematics of street-lighting optics, each a small square-ish cross-section: a dark navy sky band above a mid-blue road band with a white horizontal crosshair line, a white or pale cone of light from a luminaire at the top, and a pale elliptical light pool on the road. Groups: Luminance M (DN09, DN10, DN11, DM10, DM11, DM12, DM13, DW10, DX10); Illuminance P,C (DN50, DM50, DW50, DW52, DX50, DX51, DX52, DS50); dedicated applications (DS50, DS51, DPL1, DPL2, DSM2, DN10, DM11, DW10, DX51); night preservation (DN10, DM11, DM50, DW10, DW50, DX10, DS50, DX60, DSM11, with small star dots in the sky and smaller light pools); special user needs (DM30, DM31, DN33, DM32, DM33, DM70, DX70, Optics+BL1, Optics+BL2). A blue arrow runs from left to right under the illuminance row, labelled From narrow to wide geometries, pointing right at its end. Signify logo bottom-right: a dark circled S mark with the word signify in black.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Titles and body text dark charcoal #303034 in a geometric sans-serif (Signify Raisonne DemiBold for the title, Signify Relative Book and Bold for the rest). Title about 5.2 percent of slide height; section headings about 3.0 percent; labels about 1.5 percent; footer about 2.0 percent. Accent blue #1432FF for page number, series name and arrow. Thumbnails use navy #0B2A5B sky, mid-blue #1B5FA8 road and white light pools, with no rounded corners and no shadows.

### Slide 15

**Text**

Product specifications
15
Signify CitySoul LED gen2

**Layout**

A single centered title sits in the middle of the slide, horizontally centered and vertically at about 42 to 53 percent of the height. A small footer line with the slide number and deck name runs along the bottom-left margin at about 3.4 percent from the left edge. A Signify logo sits at the bottom-right corner. No grid or columns; no header, subtitle or body text.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed abstract gradient illustration (mesh-style colour blend, not a photograph) covering the entire slide. Its colours flow from pale mint white at the top-left corner, through deep green near the centre-top, to bright spring green at the bottom-left and a saturated electric blue at the top-right corner, with darker teal-green toward the bottom-right. There is no chart, table or diagram. One logo: the black Signify wordmark with a circled lowercase 's' emblem, placed at the bottom-right corner.

**Design**

Background is a soft multi-colour mesh gradient. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) in a geometric sans-serif display face (Signify Raisonne DemiBold), about 11 percent of slide height. Footer and page number are white (#FFFFFF) in a geometric sans-serif book weight (Signify Relative Book), about 2 percent of slide height. Accent colours are the gradient itself: pale mint #E0FFF0, deep green #005A38, bright green #00E68A, electric blue #1020E0. The logo is black (#000000). No shapes, rules, corner radii or shadows.

**Layout**

Seven technical drawings sit in two rows on a white field: four drawings across the upper row and three across the lower row, each with its caption centred beneath it. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top and 3.4% from the left edge. The footer runs along the bottom-left with the slide number at 3.4% from the left, and the Signify logo sits bottom-right near the right margin. Captions are centre-aligned under each drawing.

**Visuals**

Seven black line drawings (technical side and end elevations) of CitySoul LED gen2 luminaires on white, each with dimension callouts drawn as thin black leader lines with filled black dots at the ends. Row 1 left to right: side-entry BRP531 with width 735 and height 100; catenary or suspended BSP531 with width 630 and height 220, showing a cylindrical mounting stem with a small cap on top; post top BPP531 with width 720 and height 170, showing a bracket arm at the left; post top BGP531 for East and Graphic brackets with width 660 and height 100. Row 2 left to right: post top BVP531 for Right Curve bracket with width 630 and height 100, drawn with faint grey frame lines at the top-left and right edge; post top BGP531 76A tiltable post top with width 850 and height 170, showing a round tilt joint; BGP531 MBA + SJ for Curved with width 795 and height 100, showing a round joint and a curved housing. No photographs, no colour, no shading, no lighting effects. Drawings are unboxed and sit directly on white.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with no decoration. Title is SignifyRaisonne DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, in #303034, about 5.2% of slide height. Captions are SignifyRelative Book, a sans-serif in regular weight, #303034, about 3.0% of slide height; parenthetical sub-captions are about 2.2%. Footer slide number and text are SignifyRelative Book in #1432FF blue, about 2.0%. No shapes, borders, shadows, gradients or corner radii except the thin black line work of the drawings.

**Layout**

Two side-by-side specification tables occupy the content area below a single-line title, the left table in two columns of dark label cells and light value cells with eleven rows, the right table with ten rows. Title sits top-left at the left margin (x 3.4%) and aligns with the left table edge; a thin green bar sits above each table; the footer carries the blue page number and document name at bottom left and the Signify logo at bottom right, all on the 3.4% margin.

**Visuals**

Two specification tables, no photographs or charts. Left table: columns Label and Value, rows Type (value lists six lines: BPP530 (mini), BPP531 (large) post-top version; BRP530 (mini), BRP531 (large) side-entry version; BSP530 (mini), BSP531 (large) catenary or suspended version; BVP530 (mini), BVP531 (large) for dedicated Right Curve bracket; BGP530 (mini), BGP531 (large) for dedicated brackets and MBA version; BPP532 (mini), version with Lyre bracket), Light source (Integral LED-module), Power (CitySoul LED gen2 mini: 10.4 W - 84W; CitySoul LED gen2 large: 28W - 148W), Luminous flux (depending on LED configuration) (CitySoul LED gen2 mini: 741 - 11310 lm; CitySoul LED gen2 large: 2052W - 20640 W; or tailored flux), Luminaire efficacy (CitySoul LED gen2 mini: 44 - 181 lm / W; CitySoul LED gen2 large: 48 - 186 lm/W), L-Tune (L-Tune available for all versions), Correlated color temperature (1800K, 2200K, 2700K, 3000K, 4000K, 5700K), Color rendering index (>70, >80), Light recipes (Clear Star), Lumen maintenance at median useful life*100,000 h (L95 - L98). Right table: Control gear failure rate at median useful life*100,000 h (10%), Performance Ambient Temperature Tq (+25°C), Operating temperature range (-40 to +50°C), Mains voltage (220 – 240 V / 50 – 60 Hz), Safety Class (I & II), Driver (Programmed LED drivers), Dimming (LineSwitch, DALI, mains dimming, Dynadimmer, coded mains), Connectivity Option (Interact City, outdoor multisensor), Connectors (Nema, Zhaga), Optics (LEDgine optimized optics list of 34 codes; Ledgine-P optics list of 24 codes as printed). Logo: Signify wordmark with circled s symbol, dark grey-black, bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in SignifyRaisonne DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif with heavy weight, dark grey #303034, about 5.2% of slide height. Table label cells are filled #303034 with white (#FFFFFF) SignifyRelative Book sans-serif text at about 1.85% of slide height; value cells filled #EEEEEF with #303034 text at the same size. Thin white gaps separate rows. Green accent bars #00E38C sit above each table, flat with square corners. Footer page number and document name in blue #1432FF, sans-serif, about 2.0% of slide height. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Two equal-width tables sit side by side in two columns below a large left-aligned title, each table with a thin green bar along its top edge. The left column holds seven rows (Louvres to Weight) and the right column holds nine rows (Max SCx to Certification), with a left margin of about 3.4% and right margin of about 3.4%. The page number and blue footer label sit bottom-left, and the Signify logo sits bottom-right aligned with the right table edge.

**Visuals**

Two specification tables, no charts or photographs. Left table columns: row label (dark charcoal #303034 cell, white text) and value (light grey #EEEEEF cell, dark text). Left table rows in order: Louvres = Dedicated louvres for backlight cut-off BL1, BL2; Installation = BPP530 (mini), BPP531 (large): post-top mounting Ø 60 - 76 mm; BRP530 (mini), BPR531 (large): side-entry mounting Ø 42 - 60 mm; BSP530 (mini), BSP531 (large): catenary or suspended; BPP532 (mini): dedicated lyre (bracket available, to be ordered seperately); BVP530 (mini, BVP531 suspended with dedicated Right Curve bracket; Adjustable tilt angle: possible with BGP530/531 and specific MBA or 76PA spigot; Mounting height = BPP530 (mini): 3.5 – 6 m; BPP531 (large): 6 – 9 m; Material = Gasket: silicone rubber, heat resistant; Optics: plastic (PMMA); Cover: glass, thermally hardened; Color = Housing: Ultra dark gray (close to RAL 7022, fine textured color); Frame: Satin silver grey (RAL 9006); Other RAL and AKZO Futura colors available on request; IP rating = IP66; IK rating = IK08; Weight = Minimum 12.7 kg (mini), minimum 17.4 kg (large). Right table rows in order: Max SCx = six lines of m² values listed in the content above; Surge protection = 6/8 kV (standard) – optional 10 kV; Maintenance = housing and Service tag text; Cable options = three entries; Electrical connection = Screw connection block / Wieland push-in connector possible (optional); Accessories = For mini list and For large list; Warranty = 5 years; Certification = RoHS, REACH, CE, ENEC, ENEC+, 005. The Signify logo is a black wordmark with a circled S mark, bottom right, about 5 by 2 pixels in the file. Two thin green (#00E68A) bars run across the top of each table. None of these contain a photograph.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and body text are dark charcoal #303034; the title is a bold geometric sans in a Raisonné-style display face, large, about 5.2% of slide height. Table text is a light geometric sans (Signify Relative Book or closest Futura-like sans-serif), about 1.85% of slide height. Dark label cells #303034 with white text; value cells #EEEEEF with #303034 text; white 1px-like gutters separate rows. Accent green bars #00E68A along the top of each table; footer and page number in blue #1432FF. Square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 20

**Text**

You and Signify –
a reliable partnership
Signify is the world leader in connected LED lighting
systems, software and services. We proudly bring to
market the best lighting brands in the world, including
Signify, Philips and Interact.
We believe in close cooperation before, during, and
after every project. Our local teams provide the support
and information you need—with flexible, on-time delivery
and reliable product availability.
To help you stay ahead, the Signify Lighting Academy
offers a comprehensive range of educational resources
to grow your expertise and earn certification.
Get more info on our products, services and tools:
signify.com/installer      signify.com/specifier
20 Signify
Our global brands
Signify represents our commitment to sustainable
innovation. As our leading brand for connected lighting
systems, luminaires and components for the specification
project business, Signify solutions serve the segments
where we collaborate most closely with our customers.
Signify Interact is our IoT software platform for managing
smart lighting systems and unlocking the value of the data
they collect. Easy to install and configure, Interact
supports a wide range of applications—from small
offices to entire cities.
Philips stands for quality and energy efficiency in lighting.
It is our leading brand for innovative, off-the-shelf lighting
products for professionals and consumers alike.

**Layout**

Two equal columns split at the vertical midline: left half is a full-height green panel holding a centred title and three centred body paragraphs, a bold call-to-action and two underlined links; right half is white with a centred title and three centred brand blocks, each a logo above its paragraph. The titles sit at the top (about 6% from the top edge) and are centred over their columns. A page number '20' and the word 'Signify' sit bottom-left on the green panel, and a small Signify logo sits bottom-right on the white half, both about 5-8% from the edges.

**Visuals**

Four logos, no photographs, charts or diagrams. (1) Top right: Signify logo, a green circled 's' followed by the wordmark 'signify' in teal-green, centred above the first brand paragraph. (2) Middle right: Signify Interact logo, the teal-green 'signify' wordmark with circled 's' followed by 'interact' in dark charcoal, centred above the second paragraph. (3) Lower right: PHILIPS wordmark in bold uppercase blue, centred above the third paragraph. (4) Bottom-right corner: small black Signify logo (circled 's' with 'signify' wordmark). No other visual elements.

**Design**

Background: Split background: left half solid mint green #00E487, right half solid white #FFFFFF. Two-tone flat design: saturated mint-green panel (#00E487) on the left half and plain white on the right half, with no gradients, shadows, rules, boxes or rounded corners. Titles and body text are dark charcoal #303034. Accents are the link colour #1432FF (electric blue) used for the URLs and page footer, and the link text is underlined. Titles are set in Signify Raisonne DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, at about 5.2% of slide height (cap height about 3.6%). Body text is Signify Relative Book, a regular-weight sans-serif, about 2.8% of slide height on the left and 2.4% on the right. The bold call-to-action and URLs are Signify Raisonne DemiBold at about 2.6-2.8%. Line spacing is about 3.3% of slide height on the left body, with paragraph gaps of about 1 line.

### Slide 21

**Text**

Get into pole position
with high-performance,
energy-efficient LED
Signify is Official Lighting Partner of
Mercedes-AMG PETRONAS F1 Team
Official Lighting Partner
21
Signify ClearFlood gen2

**Layout**

Single full-bleed photograph with a three-line white headline in the upper-left, a two-line white caption at lower-left, and a logo lockup with a small 'Official Lighting Partner' label at lower-right. The headline and caption share a left margin of about 4.1% of slide width; the page number and footer sit at the bottom-left at about 92.5% height, and there is no conventional title placeholder or grid.

**Visuals**

Photograph, full-bleed, covering the whole slide. Subject: a black Mercedes-AMG PETRONAS F1 race car parked in a dark garage bay, seen in side profile facing left, with its front wing at lower-left and rear wing at upper-right. Action: static display shot, car lit from above. Composition: wide landscape frame; the car sits in the lower-right third, a large glowing rectangular LED panel fills the upper-right quadrant at a tilted angle, and the garage roll-up doors fill the middle background. Lighting: the overhead LED panel is near-white and dominant, casting cool teal and cyan light onto the car, the floor and the reflective wet-looking concrete floor below the car. Dominant colours: near-black, deep teal, cyan, white. Not cropped at the edges; no border. Overlay: left side and top-left are darkened to near-black to support the white headline. Sponsor livery visible on the car: PETRONAS, IWC, Pirelli, Solera, Teamviewer, Signify, AMD, Snapdragon and Mercedes-AMG marks. Logo lockup at lower-right: green Signify wordmark with its circular 's' mark (about #2ED36E), a thin white vertical divider, the white Mercedes three-pointed star in a circle, and the white stacked AMG PETRONAS FORMULA ONE TEAM logo with the AMG slash mark above the word PETRONAS.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of a dark garage with a glowing white LED panel and teal-tinted floor (photo covers the slide; underlying solid fill #FFFFFF is hidden). Dark cinematic photographic look with a near-black background and white text. Title: white (#FFFFFF) geometric sans-serif, SignifyRaisonne DemiBold, medium-large weight, about 5.9% of slide height per line, left aligned, line spacing about 6.3% of height. Body/caption: white (#FFFFFF) SignifyRelative Book sans-serif, regular weight, about 2.8% of slide height. Accents: Signify green (#2ED36E) in the logo; page number and footer text in blue (#1432FF). Line and shape styles: a thin white vertical divider line in the logo lockup; no rules, boxes or corner radii. Shadows and gradients: a dark vignette gradient darkens the left side; the LED panel has a soft cyan glow.

### Slide 22

**Text**

Signify
the meaning of light
10-2025 | v1

**Layout**

A single centred lockup sits in the middle of the slide: the Signify wordmark on top, the tagline below it, both centred horizontally with no title placeholder, grid or columns. The date line sits alone in the bottom-right corner with a margin of about 2% from the right and bottom edges. No slide number or footer rule is visible.

**Visuals**

Logo: the Signify wordmark, consisting of a ring-shaped icon with a lowercase s inside it, followed by the letters ignify set in a monospace-style geometric typeface with slab-like terminals on the i and the f. The ring is a thick green stroke (about 0.9% of slide height), circular, with a small gap at the top centre. The wordmark has a left-to-right green-to-teal gradient, from green on the s and i to teal on the f and y. No photographs, charts, diagrams or other illustrations are present.

**Design**

Background: Solid charcoal #303034 covering the whole slide. Flat dark charcoal background with no gradient, texture or pattern. The wordmark uses a green-to-teal gradient (#05E089 at the left, #1BC9C6 at the right). The tagline is light grey in a regular-weight geometric sans-serif (SignifyRelative-Book style), about 5% of slide height, set in lowercase. The date line is small grey text, about 2.2% of slide height, in the same sans family. No shadows, borders or corner radii are visible. The wordmark's cap-height is roughly the height of the ring, about 17% of slide height.
